- **LLY** · **TERMINATED** · [NCT05668962](https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT05668962) P2 · TERMINATED · primary completion 2026-01-13 — Restor. I-131 Upt. + Selpercatinib in RET F-P RAI-R TC (Thyroid Cancer, Thyroid Carcinoma, Metastatic Thyroid Cancer)
- **NVO** · **RESULTS POSTED** · [NCT04775082](https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT04775082) P3 · ACTIVE_NOT_RECRUITING · primary completion 2023-08-01 — SCALE KIDS: Research Study to Look at How Well a New Medicine is at Lowering Weight in Children With Obesity (Obesity)
- **NVO** · **RESULTS POSTED** · [NCT06588504](https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT06588504) P3 · COMPLETED · primary completion 2025-07-20 — Research Study in Japan to Compare Dasiglucagon With Glucagon in Treating Very Low Levels of Blood Sugar in Asian Adults With Type 1 Diabetes and Testing of Dasiglucagon for the Same Condition in Japanese Adolescents (Diabetes Mellitus, Type 1)
- **MRK** · **TERMINATED** · [NCT04428151](https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT04428151) P2 · TERMINATED · primary completion 2024-05-31 — Lenvatinib (E7080/MK-7902) in Combination With Pembrolizumab (MK-3475) vs. Standard Chemotherapy and Lenvatinib Monotherapy in Participants With Recurrent/Metastatic Head and Neck Squamous Cell Carcinoma That Progressed After Platinum Therapy and Immunotherapy (MK-7902-009/E7080-G000-228/LEAP-009) (Squamous Cell Carcinoma of Head and Neck)
- **BMY** · **NEW PHASE 3** · [NCT07940777](https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT07940777) P3 · NOT_YET_RECRUITING · primary completion 2029-07-21 — A Study to Evaluate the Safety and Efficacy of Mavacamten in Adults With Symptomatic Non-obstructive Hypertrophic Cardiomyopathy (SOLSTICE-HCM) (Non-obstructive Hypertrophic Cardiomyopathy)
